# JEE Main Mathematics - Application of Derivatives

## Topic 1: Tangent Normal

**Total Questions:** 63

### Q1. JEE Main 2025 (3 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The shortest distance between the curves $y^2=8 \mathrm{x}$ and $x^2+y^2+12 y+35=0$ is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** $2 \sqrt{3}-1$
- **(B)** $\sqrt{2}$
- **(C)** $3 \sqrt{2}-1$
- **(D)** $2 \sqrt{2}-1$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $2 \sqrt{2}-1$**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/l01wWOs9K8Oebu8nCRXy0ID5sTT3DsHDSR3CxRi0xoo.original.fullsize.png"/>
Equation of normal to parabola
$y^2=8 x \text { is } y=m x-4 m-2 m^3$
passes through $(0,-6)$ we get
$\begin{aligned}
& -6=-4 \mathrm{~m}-2 \mathrm{~m}^3 \\
& \Rightarrow \mathrm{~m}^3+2 \mathrm{~m}-3=0 \\
& \Rightarrow(\mathrm{~m}-1)\left(\mathrm{m}^2+\mathrm{m}+3\right)=0 \Rightarrow \mathrm{~m}=-1 \\
& \mathrm{P}=\left(\mathrm{am}^2,-2 \mathrm{am}\right)=(2,-4) \\
& \therefore \text { Shortest distance }=\mathrm{PC}-\mathrm{r} \\
& =(2 \sqrt{2}-1)
\end{aligned}$

</details>

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, the equation of curve is <math Point in first quadrant is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now finding slope of tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Slope of normal <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, equation of normal will be, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>- intercept <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>11</mn></math></p></blockquote>

### Q3. JEE Main 2023 (06 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

<p>Let a curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> pass through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the tangent at any point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math> to the given curve cuts the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-axis at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math> is equal to _____.</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>A curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> pass through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>,</p><p>And the tangent at any point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math> to the given curve cuts the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-axis at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math></p><p>Now tangent to the curve is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>X</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Now at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Y</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis, put <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>X</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>x</mi></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>x</mi></math> as point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> given,</p><p>Now given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> {here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math> on the curve}</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>d</mi><mfenced><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p>Now integrating both side we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math>, </p><p>Now given curve passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>, so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>So, equation of curve will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now curve also passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, by distance formula we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p>Now finding intersection of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>16</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, </p><p>Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> lies on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo></math> so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> will not satisfy,</p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>16</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>256</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>292</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q5. JEE Main 2023 (30 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The number of points on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>54</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>135</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>70</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>180</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>210</mn><mi>x</mi></math> at which the normal lines are parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>90</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given curve is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>54</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>135</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>70</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>180</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>210</mn><mi>x</mi></math></p><p>Normal lines on this curve, are parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>90</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, hence slope of normal is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>90</mn></mfrac></math>, so slope of tangent to the given curve is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>m</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>90</mn></math>.</p><p>Slope of tangent on the given curve is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>270</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>540</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>210</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>360</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>210</mn></math></p><p>Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>90</mn></math>   </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>270</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>540</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>210</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>360</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>210</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>90</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>270</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>540</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>210</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>360</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>120</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>27</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>54</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, there are four points.</p>

### Q6. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If the equation of the normal to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to ______</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given curve is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>At point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, we have</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></math></p><p>Differentiating <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> both sides w.r.t. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> slope of normal is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>, hence</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q7. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is also tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to ______.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **195**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>Curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></math></p><p>Now differentiating to find slope at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msubsup><mi>y</mi><mi>P</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn></math></p><p>Now tangent to curve at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>45</mn></math></p><p>Now the tangent is also tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></math> and passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> so plottnig the diagram we get,</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/52e99a10-5410-486b-b243-a518c434b43e-image.png" style="width: 320px; height: 140px;" /></p><p>Now differentiating the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><menclose notation="right"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></menclose><mrow><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>So, equating with tangent slope we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now from curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></math></p><p>So, at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>21</mn></math></p><p>Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>21</mn></math> are satisfying the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>45</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>21</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>195</mn></math></p><p>Note at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>3</mn></math> tangent will be parallel so it is rejected</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q8. JEE Main 2022 (27 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math> be the number of points on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, where the tangents to the curve are parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-axis, respectively. Then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>N</mi></math> equals _______.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Now differentiating both side we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (for horizontal tangent)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>, which does not satisfy the original equation so <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>.</p><p>As <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mtext>which&#160;is&#160;a&#160;false&#160;statement</mtext></mfenced></math> </p><p>Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  (from equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math> for vertical tangent)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (putting the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math> from equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mn>45</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>36</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>So at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msup><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></mstyle><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>)</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q9. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let the area enclosed by the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis, and the tangent and normal drawn to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>. Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mi>A</mi></math> is equal to _______.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **170**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>The given curve is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> </p><p>On differentiating both sides, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>y</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now, at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>48</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>48</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>So slope of tangent <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mi>T</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and </p><p>slope of normal <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mi>N</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>i.e. equation of tangent <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p>and equation of normal <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>31</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Intersection point of tangent and normal is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>The intersection point of tangent and normal with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis respectively will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>31</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, area of triangle formed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo></math>Base<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#215;</mo></math>Height</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>31</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mfrac><mn>85</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>85</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>170</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q10. JEE Main 2022 (28 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> be a line which is normal to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> on the curve. If the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> lies on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is origin, then the area of the triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> is equal to _____.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **13**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The given curve is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/186c0d68-a71b-481b-8805-541414015d9e-image.png" style="width: 300px; height: 197px;" /></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then normal at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Since this normal passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Also  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p>So <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now area of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q11. JEE Main 2022 (26 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi></math> be the set of all the natural numbers, for which the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> is a tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> Then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#981;</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mfenced><mi>S</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Differentiating, we get, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8901;</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mfenced close="|" open=""><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>So, equation of the tangent to the curve at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>N</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q12. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the angle made by the tangent at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>, with the positive <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>48</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>27</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, </p><p>Curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p>Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mstyle></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mi>d</mi><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mi>d</mi><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mstyle><mfenced><mrow><mn>12</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mstyle><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>tan</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>12</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Now putting <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p>as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> lie on curve</p><p>So <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q13. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, is the tangent to the parabola <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>V</mi></math> is the vertex of the parabola, then the slope of the line through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>V</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>26</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>23</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Plotting the given data we have, </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f523aafe-bc8f-4fab-9cfc-298454c2cf28-image.png" style="width: 200px; height: 171px;" /></p><p>Now by diagram, Slope of tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo></math> Slope of line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AP</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>On solving we get  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, slope of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>V</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q14. JEE Main 2022 (24 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the tangent at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math> passes through the origin, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> does NOT lie on the curve

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>81</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p>Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi></math></p><p>So at point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mrow><mi>at</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math></p><p>Also slope of line in two point form will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math></p><p>Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> lie on curve</p><p>We get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>iii</mi></mfenced></math></p><p>From equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math> we get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> (as slope is equal)</p><p>Now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>15</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mrow/><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>15</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> does not intersect the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q15. JEE Main 2021 (31 Aug Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An angle of intersection of the curves, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo></math> is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given equations are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></math>where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mi>b</mi></math>.</p><p>From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p>Substituting value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math> in the above equation</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>b</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p>Substituting value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math>, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
Point of intersection is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> be the angle between curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math>.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>tan</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mfenced><mrow><mi>tan</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"></math>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msqrt><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math></p>

### Q16. JEE Main 2021 (25 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let a curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> pass through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>log</mi><mi>e</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> and have slope <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><msub><mi>log</mi><mi>e</mi></msub><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> for all positive real values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>.</mo></math> Then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>e</mi></mfenced></math> is equal to _____.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let a curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> pass through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>log</mi><mi>e</mi></msub><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> and have slope <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><msub><mi>log</mi><mi>e</mi></msub><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> for all positive real values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>. <p>Therefore, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>ln</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mi>y</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mi>y</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math></p><p>Put <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=2</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#8467;</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>e</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q17. JEE Main 2021 (26 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let the normals at all the points on a given curve pass through a fixed point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the curve passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>, given that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to _____.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **9**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>All normals of circle passes through centre</p><p>Radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><msup><mi>B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2598bbc5-772a-4249-8672-aa02dd26749d-image.png" style="width: 270px; height: 170px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>given that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p>

</details>

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math></p><p>By solving equations, we get  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>5</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Also
