xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>k</mi><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Curve cut orthogonally</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>k</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> from <math ### Q19. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The shortest distance between the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/54515637-83b7-43bd-9435-f7186d2c3362-image.png" style="width: 285px; height: 210px;" /></p><p>Shortest distance between curves is always along common normal.</p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> be any point on the parabola <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></math>.</p><p>Clearly, slope of line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>. </p><p>Hence, slope of perpendicular line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo></math>Slope of tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> </p><p>Therefore, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>P</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo></math>Slope of normal<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Shortest distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q20. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the area of the triangle formed by the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis, the normal and the tangent to the circle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn><mi>A</mi></math> is equal to _________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1225**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d04ae13e-7374-404b-95d0-75590cc9e725-image.png" style="width: 320px; height: 250px;" /></p><p>Equation of normal</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>and equation of tangents</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>43</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Area of triangle<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>43</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mn>7</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>172</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>12</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1225</mn><mn>24</mn></mfrac></math> Sq. unit</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1225</mn></math></p><p>Note: As positive <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis was given in the question so question was bonus in JEE Mains.</p>

</details>

### Q21. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and the tangent line to this curve at origin is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the possible values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi></math> are:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><msub><mo>|</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msub><mo>|</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Curve passes through origin</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q22. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

For which of the following curves, the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> is the tangent at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>x</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> (incorrect)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>m</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>24</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>24</mn></mfrac></math> (incorrect)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn></math> (correct)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>4</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math> (incorrect)</p>

</details>

### Q23. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> meets the curve again at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the ordinate of the point which divides <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> internally in the ratio <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Slope of tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></msub></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msub><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p>So equation tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>:</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
for point of intersection with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>t</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>So for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
ordinate of required point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q24. JEE Main 2020 (06 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the tangent to the curve,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>log</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>,</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is parallel to the line-segment joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>,then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math> is equal to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>&#39;</mo><mfenced><mi>c</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>e</mi><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="script">l</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q25. JEE Main 2020 (05 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math> touch the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math> at the points where the curve intersects the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8230;</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Therefore, points are<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q26. JEE Main 2020 (05 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Which of the following points lies on the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>y</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>at (1,0)
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
equation of tangent at (1,0) is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
so option (3) is correct</p>

</details>

### Q27. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> and the normal to the parabola <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> intersect at the same point on the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is .....

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For the parabola <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>So, equation of normal is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> intersects <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Given curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></math></p><p>So, equation of tangent to the curve at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q28. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The equation of the normal to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>8</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>ln</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>ln</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#183;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math></p><p>Now at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p>

### Q29. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> be a point on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, nearest to the line, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>. Then the equation of the normal to the curve at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>62</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3e6159cc-b791-4b00-81ba-1b819e2b9dcb-image.png" style="width: 254px; height: 212px;" /></p><p>Tangent at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> will be parallel to given line</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> lies on curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p>For normal at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Slope of normal<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>(Negative inverse of slope of tangent)
Equation of normal : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q30. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>y</mi></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is parallel to the line joining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>y</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>y</mi><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Now for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>As the tangent is parallel to the line joining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mstyle><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>I</mi></math></p><p>Point lie on curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q31. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the curves, <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> touch each other at a point, then the largest value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is ____________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **36**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>If general equation of circle is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>f</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> then its center is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and radius is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><msup><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></msqrt></math></p><p>Two circles touches each other if <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#177;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Distance between <math><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi></mi><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is either  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mi>k</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>o</mi><mi>r</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>k</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msqrt><mi>k</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>k</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>  </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Maximum value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is <math><mn>36</mn></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q32. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The length of the perpendicular from the origin, on normal to the curve, <math><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, at the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, is.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Differentiate the above curve,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>So, at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>&#39;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.</p><p>So, equation of normal to the curve at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>.</p><p>Thus, perpendicular distance from origin on the normal is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q33. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let the normal at a point <math><mi>P</mi></math> on the curve <math><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> intersect the <math><mi>y</mi></math> -axis at <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mi>m</mi></math> is the slope of the tangent at <math><mi>P</mi></math> to the curve, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to ___________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p><math><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8201;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>y</mi><mi>&#39;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>&#39;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>&#39;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#8201;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#8201;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Slope of tangent <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#8201;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q34. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on it is parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The slope of a line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> hence the slope of the given line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Also, we know that the slope of tangent to a curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></msub></math></p><p>We have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Applying quotient rule for differentiation, i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>d</mi><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mfrac><mi>u</mi><mi>v</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>v</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>u</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mstyle></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mstyle></mfenced></mrow><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#183;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math></p><p>Also, we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mrow><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math ### Q35. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>S</mi></math> be the set of all values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> for which the tangent to the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is parallel to the line segment joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>S</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>The slope of a line joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Thus, the slope of the line segment joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Given, this line segment is parallel to the tangent of the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and we know that the slope of the tangent to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> also the slope of two parallel lines is same, hence </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, the set <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q36. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the tangent to the curve, <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>b</mi></math> at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is perpendicular to the line, <math><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then which one of the following points lies on the curve?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> it passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Slope of the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> hence, slope of the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>We know that if two lines are perpendicular, then the product of their slopes is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Slope of tangent of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo></math> which is perpendicular to <math><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Also, we know that the slop of tangent to a curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> at a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mfenced><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mi>&#39;</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>And, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> we get <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>At, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, out of the given options only <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfy the curve, hence this point will lie on the curve.</p>

</details>

### Q37. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Given that the slope of the tangent to a curve <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> at any point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>. If the curve passes through the centre of the circle <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then its equation is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Slope of tangent, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Integrating both sides, we get,
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> it passes through centre <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> of given circle,
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> solution <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q38. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The shortest distance between the line <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math> and the curve <math><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/5613f359-4940-49c6-9c65-a2f7f0518a57-image1.png" />
<math><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Differentiating w.r.t. <math><mi>x</mi></math> we get,
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&#39;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#39;</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8201;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
For the shortest distance, the tangent at point <math><mi>P</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math> will be parallel to the given line
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>&#39;</mi><msub><mrow><mo>|</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8757;</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
The shortest distance between the given curve &amp; the line
= The perpendicular distance of point <math><mi>P</mi></math> from the line
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>
